The source revision is independent of the PR base: this maps `agent/` at `a9da325e274a70a249239d1f95913495797780ee`, while the evidence-only change starts at the specified project branch base. All source and audit links below are pinned to the source revision. The source column, carrying line, character span and verbatim cell together identify each occurrence; repeated occurrences have separate IDs. No `agent/` file, task record, executable helper, product repository or Otto guidance file is modified by OT-15.

## Assignment decisions

1. **Routing and model bindings have one Otto home:** `guidance/run-contract.md` → **Routing and bindings**. The source authority is [D18 L11–26](https://github.com/banodoco/astrid-dev/blob/a9da325e274a70a249239d1f95913495797780ee/agent/shared/run-contract.md#L11); the full table, high-judgment binding and exceptions map there. Other rows that refer to routing also resolve there. Retained Dev examples and historical audits are not a second Otto binding authority.
2. **ChatGPT session modes have one Otto home:** `guidance/run-contract.md` → **ChatGPT session modes**. The source authority is [D18 L30–38](https://github.com/banodoco/astrid-dev/blob/a9da325e274a70a249239d1f95913495797780ee/agent/shared/run-contract.md#L30). Its web-session restriction, hardest-card rule, T-route qualification, unavailable-mode handling and escalation condition stay attached. Other target documents link to that section instead of defining another mode table.
3. **Core rows carry reusable role, authority, planning, bounded dispatch, coordination, evidence, review or guidance-authoring rules.** A target is a relocation destination, not a rewritten instruction or new permission. Where several source occurrences share a target, each occurrence remains separately mapped; the map does not fuse their wording or authorize deletion of their conditions.
4. **Dev rows stay at the exact source spans stated.** Release gates and promotion rules, GPU/product environments, named repository fixtures and commands, Dev run state, lab workflows, generated Dev navigation and migration-specific packaging remain local. The populated `agent/shared/templates/run.yaml` is entirely Dev-owned; the run-state location does not become an Otto state engine. A mixed sentence that cannot be lifted with its qualifications stays Dev in full.
5. **Older audits are provenance, not current authority.** Plan 73 line numbers describe an older candidate. This map uses current complete clauses at the source pin; the historical crosswalk below records replacements rather than reviving earlier permissions. Targets and headings are the Step 1 set approved for OT-16/OT-17. An approved heading with no inherited row is not permission to invent a rule to fill it.

## Counting and transcription method

The table intentionally includes contextual lead-ins, table headers, frontmatter and operational template/code lines as source units so a condition is not lost by filtering only for imperative words. Each prose unit is a complete source sentence (semicolon-linked conditions remain together); each source table row remains one row; each nonblank template/code line is retained. These are evidence units, not a claim that each unit is an independent logical obligation. Headings remain the source context in every row; blank lines, fence delimiters, frontmatter delimiters and layout-only region boundaries are not independent rows. Normative headings such as “Never” or “only when the owner asks” qualify the rows beneath them and must travel with those rows.

The verbatim cell contains the literal current source span, including Markdown punctuation, link destinations and qualifiers. HTML numeric entities are only table-transport escaping; decoding them yields the source characters. The linked carrying line supplies the surrounding sentences, list scope, antecedents and exceptions. **Do not transplant an isolated sentence while dropping its carrying-line or heading conditions.** Old source paths inside quotations are evidence, not links or proposed Otto filenames. Link rebasing and target authorship belong to the later packets; no source rule is paraphrased here.

Rule IDs have the form `D18-L024-03`: document order, current one-based source line, and occurrence order on that line. Character spans are one-based inclusive positions in that source line. The inventory reports all mapped units, the two dispositions, and a reproducible lexical aid: case-insensitive whole-word occurrences of `must|never|only|requires|permissions?|review|routing|proof|authority` in rendered unit text, with Markdown link destinations omitted. “Keyword units” counts rows containing at least one such word; “keyword hits” counts occurrences. This aid does not decide whether an unmarked imperative is consequential and does not count headings twice. Current totals are **706 keyword units / 884 keyword hits** across **2955 mapped units**. The orchestrator's independent per-document rule count remains the required proof; neither this lexical aid nor the historical excerpt count substitutes for it.

AR/AS comments and old implementation/test citations do not prove the current repository. The reconciliation appendix accounts for historical nonidentical and noncurrent rows separately.
